dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003ethe dependency constraints their extensions require at Quarkus application build and run times (typically in the form of a Maven BOM artifact);\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003ea list of Maven \u003ccode\u003etest-jar\u003c/code\u003e artifacts that should be added to the Quarkus platform integration testsuite (it would typically be some of the original tests from the extension project that are released\nalong with the extension as Maven \u003ccode\u003etest-jar\u003c/code\u003e artifacts).\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eThe Quarkus platform BOM generator will perform an alignment across all the platform member dependency constraints and will generate a BOM for each platform member\nthat is compatible with all the other platform members.\nOnce all the member BOMs have been generated, the platform integration testsuite (consisting of the tests contributed by all the members) will be run against the generated platform BOMs.\nAs long as \u003cem\u003eall\u003c/em\u003e the platform integration tests pass, the generated platform member BOMs (and a few other related artifacts), representing a single compatible development stack, can be released.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch3 Applications developed in compliance with a Quarkus platform will be importing platform member BOMs that belong to the same platform release.\nE.g. a platform may include extensions from \u003ca href=\"https://github.com/apache/camel-quarkus\"\u003eCamel Quarkus\u003c/a\u003e and \u003ca href=\"https://github.com/kiegroup/kogito-runtimes\"\u003eKogito Runtimes\u003c/a\u003e, in which case a platform release will include a \u003ccode\u003equarkus-camel-bom\u003c/code\u003e\nand a \u003ccode\u003equarkus-kogito-bom\u003c/code\u003e. Applications using Camel extensions will then be importing the \u003ccode\u003equarkus-camel-bom\u003c/code\u003e, applications using Kogito extensions will be importing the \u003ccode\u003equarkus-kogito-bom\u003c/code\u003e and applications using Camel and Kogito extensions\nat the same time will be importing both the \u003ccode\u003equarkus-camel-bom\u003c/code\u003e and the \u003ccode\u003equarkus-kogito-bom\u003c/code\u003e.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eNOTE\u003c/strong\u003e The ordering of member BOM imports in applications includes information about the platform release. This information is used by the Quarkus application bootstrap mechanism to make sure the platform member BOMs imported by the application belong to the same\nplatform release and fail the build or log a warning in case that is not the case. This is done to make sure application developers do not import member BOMs that belong different platform releases by mistake.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch2 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003eBuilding and testing the platform project\u003c/h2\u003e\u003ca id=\"user-content-building-and-testing-the-platform-project\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: Building and testing the platform project\" href=\"#building-and-testing-the-platform-project\"\u003e\u003csvg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"\u003e\u003cpath d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eThe platform is currently configured in a single Maven POM file (the root \u003ccode\u003epom.xml\u003c/code\u003e) with the exception of a few additional resource files. This POM file includes a few Maven plugin configuration generating the platform artifacts and a \u003ccode\u003eplatformConfig\u003c/code\u003e configuration.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eThe additional resource files are:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003ccode\u003esrc/main/resources/extensions-overrides.json\u003c/code\u003e — overrides extension metadata in the generated JSON descriptors (for example, marking an extension as unlisted). It is wired in through the \u003ccode\u003edescriptorGenerator/overridesFile\u003c/code\u003e element;\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003ccode\u003esrc/main/resources/xslt/\u003c/code\u003e — XSL templates applied to generated test POMs, referenced from the test configuration \u003ccode\u003etransformWith\u003c/code\u003e element.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eIMPORTANT\u003c/strong\u003e Maven build process launched from the root project directory will generate the complete platform Maven project (during the \u003ccode\u003eprocess-resources\u003c/code\u003e phase) in the \u003ccode\u003egenerated-platform-project\u003c/code\u003e directory. The generated platform project\nshould not be modified manually except for local testing purposes. The project will be re-generated on every platform build launched from the root platform project directory.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eThe \u003ccode\u003egenerated-platform-project\u003c/code\u003e will refer to the root platform project \u003ccode\u003epom.xml\u003c/code\u003e as its parent POM and so may inherit properties and other common configuration from it.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch3 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003eGenerating the platform project\u003c/h3\u003e\u003ca id=\"user-content-generating-the-platform-project\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: Generating the platform project\" href=\"#generating-the-platform-project\"\u003e\u003csvg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"\u003e\u003cpath d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ccode\u003e./mvnw -Dsync\u003c/code\u003e will generate the complete platform project.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eThe platform project will typically be generated on every build anyway. But this command could be used in case you want to simply (re-)generate the platform project w/o running any other commands on it.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eNOTE\u003c/strong\u003e the way it's currently done is any command launched from the platform project's root dir will be passed to the \u003ccode\u003egenerated-platform-project\u003c/code\u003e, which means \u003ccode\u003e./mvnw -Dsync\u003c/code\u003e will not only generate the platform project but will also be executed against it.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eIMPORTANT\u003c/strong\u003e the contents of \u003ccode\u003egenerated-platform-project\u003c/code\u003e are committed to the repository. After changing the root \u003ccode\u003epom.xml\u003c/code\u003e, run \u003ccode\u003e./mvnw -Dsync\u003c/code\u003e and commit the regenerated files along with the change. CI runs the generation separately and fails the build if the committed \u003ccode\u003egenerated-platform-project\u003c/code\u003e differs from what the current configuration produces.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch3 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003eInstalling the platform\u003c/h3\u003e\u003ca class=\"pl-ent\"\u003etest\u003c/span\u003e\u0026gt;\n...\u003c/pre\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003col dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eName that is used to refer to the member in the POMs and other generated resources\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eThe original member BOM coordinates representing members build and run times classpath constraints\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eWhether the member actually participates in the platform build or not (if a member is disabled, the rest of its configuration will be ignored)\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eThe coordinates under which the generated member platform BOM should be installed and deployed\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eThe BOM generator detects whether the generated member BOM has changed since the previous release and records the value of the last release in which the BOM has actually changed. The value of this element is meant to be updated during the release process by the tool itself in case the BOM has actually changed.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eCustom dependency management configuration\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eWhether to align constraints of the member BOM that appear to be originating from the same project on a common/preferred version of that project or keep the original member BOM, the default for this option is false\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eWhether to keep exclusions found in the member BOM for thirdparty dependencies shared with other members. This option allows each member to have its own exclusions in their BOMs for common dependencies. The default value is true.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eMaven artifact groupIds the extension artifacts of this member will have. If not configured, the original member BOM groupId will be the default. This helps optimize identify extension version constraints in the member BOM.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eMaven artifact groupIds that member has the exclusive right to manage in the platform that other members will not be allowed to override.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eThe default configuration options for all the tests of the member\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eCan be used to skip all the member tests (unless explicitly overridden in a test config)\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eSpecific test configuration will reference a Maven test jar artifact containing the tests that should be run as part of the platform's IT\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eWhether the test should be skipped during the platform IT run\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ol\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" tree.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eNOTE\u003c/strong\u003e a property reference that is meant to end up \u003cem\u003eunresolved\u003c/em\u003e in the generated test POM \u003ccode\u003epom.xml\u003c/code\u003e. \u003ccode\u003edependencyManagement\u003c/code\u003e are prepended to that member's input constraints, \u003cem\u003ebefore\u003c/em\u003e its own \u003ccode\u003ebom\u003c/code\u003e is imported. They therefore both \u003cstrong\u003eadd\u003c/strong\u003e constraints the member BOM does not have and \u003cstrong\u003eoverride\u003c/strong\u003e versions that it does have.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eThree syntactic forms are accepted:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"highlight highlight-text-xml notranslate position-relative overflow-auto\" dir=\"auto\" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content=\"  \u0026lt;dependencyManagement\u0026gt;\n    \u0026lt;!-- 1. plain coordinates --\u0026gt;\n class=\"pl-ent\"\u003edependencyManagement\u003c/span\u003e\u0026gt;\n    \u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u0026lt;!--\u003c/span\u003e 1. plain coordinates \u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e--\u0026gt;\u003c/span\u003e\u003c/span\u003e\n    \u0026lt;\u003cspan class=\"pl-ent\"\u003edependency\u003c/span\u003e\u0026gt;org.acme:acme:${acme.version}\u0026lt;/\u003cspan class=\"pl-ent\"\u003edependency\u003c/span\u003e\u0026gt;\n\n    \u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u0026lt;!--\u003c/span\u003e 2. expanded form, supports scope and exclusions \u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e--\u0026gt;\u003c/span\u003e\u003c/span\u003e\n    \u0026lt;\u003cspan class=\"pl-ent\"\u003edependencySpec\u003c/span\u003e\u0026gt;\n      \u0026lt;\u003cspan class=\"pl-ent\"\u003eartifact\u003c/span\u003e\u0026gt;org.acme:foo:${acme.version}\u0026lt;/\u003cspan class=\"pl-ent\"\u003eartifact\u003c/span\u003e\u0026gt;\n      \u0026lt;\u003cspan class=\"pl-ent\"\u003eexclusions\u003c/span\u003e\u0026gt;\n        \u0026lt;\u003cspan class=\"pl-ent\"\u003eexclusion\u003c/span\u003e\u0026gt;org.acme:bar\u0026lt;/\u003cspan class=\"pl-ent\"\u003edependencySpec\u003c/span\u003e\u0026gt;\n\n    \u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u0026lt;!--\u003c/span\u003e 3. importing a third-party BOM \u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e--\u0026gt;\u003c/span\u003e\u003c/span\u003e\n    \u0026lt;\u003cspan class=\"pl-ent\"\u003edependencySpec\u003c/span\u003e\u0026gt;\n      \u0026lt;\u003cspan class=\"pl-ent\"\u003eartifact\u003c/span\u003e\u0026gt;com.third-party:their-bom::pom:2.0\u0026lt;/\u003cspan class=\"pl-ent\"\u003eartifact\u003c/span\u003e\u0026gt;\n      \u0026lt;\u003cspan class=\"pl-ent\"\u003escope\u003c/span\u003e\u0026gt;import\u0026lt;/\u003cspan class=\"pl-ent\"\u003escope\u003c/span\u003e\u0026gt;\n    \u0026lt;/\u003cspan class=\"pl-ent\"\u003edependencySpec\u003c/span\u003e\u0026gt;\n  \u0026lt;/\u003cspan class=\"pl-ent\"\u003edependencyManagement\u003c/span\u003e\u0026gt;\u003c/pre\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eAn imported BOM is expanded, so every constraint it manages is merged into the member's input constraints. This is the preferred way to move a whole third-party dependency set (e.g. Jackson) ahead of the version Quarkus core ships — configure it on the \u003ccode\u003ecore\u003c/code\u003e member. It is equivalent to, but much less brittle than, listing every single artifact under \u003ccode\u003eenforcedDependencies\u003c/code\u003e (see below).\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eIMPORTANT\u003c/strong\u003e the \u003ccode\u003eartifact\u003c/code\u003e element is parsed as \u003ccode\u003egroupId:artifactId:classifier:type:version\u003c/code\u003e. The accepted shorter forms are \u003ccode\u003egroupId:artifactId:version\u003c/code\u003e and \u003ccode\u003egroupId:artifactId:classifier:version\u003c/code\u003e — note that a four-segment string sets the \u003cem\u003eclassifier\u003c/em\u003e, not the type. A BOM import therefore has to use the five-segment form with an empty classifier: \u003ccode\u003ecom.third-party:their-bom::pom:2.0\u003c/code\u003e. Writing \u003ccode\u003ecom.third-party:their-bom:pom:2.0\u003c/code\u003e would try to resolve \u003ccode\u003etheir-bom-2.0-pom.jar\u003c/code\u003e and fail.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eNOTE\u003c/strong\u003e constraints pulled in through a BOM import are still subject to the normal alignment rules described in \u003ca href=\"#platform-bom-generation\"\u003ePlatform BOM generation\u003c/a\u003e — the \u003ccode\u003eio.quarkus:quarkus-bom\u003c/code\u003e constraints win, and across members the newer version of a given origin is preferred. Importing an \u003cem\u003eolder\u003c/em\u003e third-party BOM will therefore not necessarily downgrade an artifact that another member manages at a newer version.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eThe expanded form can also be spelled as a \u003ccode\u003edependencies\u003c/code\u003e/\u003ccode\u003edependency\u003c/code\u003e list:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"highlight highlight-text-xml notranslate position-relative overflow-auto\" dir=\"auto\" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content=\"  \u0026lt;dependencyManagement\u0026gt;\n    \u0026lt;dependencies\u0026gt;\n      \u0026lt;dependency\u0026gt;\n        \u0026lt;artifact\u0026gt;com.third-party:their-bom::pom:2.0\u0026lt;/artifact\u0026gt;\n        \u0026lt;scope\u0026gt;import\u0026lt;/scope\u0026gt;\n      \u0026lt;/dependency\u0026gt;\n    \u0026lt;/dependencies\u0026gt;\n  \u0026lt;/dependencyManagement\u0026gt;\"\u003e\u003cpre\u003e  \u0026lt;\u003cspan class=\"pl-ent\"\u003edependencyManagement\u003c/span\u003e\u0026gt;\n    \u0026lt;\u003cspan class=\"pl-ent\"\u003edependencies\u003c/span\u003e\u0026gt;\n      \u0026lt;\u003cspan class=\"pl-ent\"\u003edependency\u003c/span\u003e\u0026gt;\n        \u0026lt;\u003cspan class=\"pl-ent\"\u003eartifact\u003c/span\u003e\u0026gt;com.third-party:their-bom::pom:2.0\u0026lt;/\u003cspan class=\"pl-ent\"\u003eartifact\u003c/span\u003e\u0026gt;\n        \u0026lt;\u003cspan class=\"pl-ent\"\u003escope\u003c/span\u003e\u0026gt;import\u0026lt;/\u003cspan class=\"pl-ent\"\u003escope\u003c/span\u003e\u0026gt;\n      \u0026lt;/\u003cspan class=\"pl-ent\"\u003edependency\u003c/span\u003e\u0026gt;\n    \u0026lt;/\u003cspan class=\"pl-ent\"\u003edependencies\u003c/span\u003e\u0026gt;\n  \u0026lt;/\u003cspan class=\"pl-ent\"\u003edependencyManagement\u003c/span\u003e\u0026gt;\u003c/pre\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eIMPORTANT\u003c/strong\u003e this spelling \u003cem\u003ereplaces\u003c/em\u003e the whole constraint list instead of appending to it, so any plain \u003ccode\u003edependency\u003c/code\u003e elements configured before it are silently dropped — the build still succeeds, the constraints simply disappear from the generated BOM. Prefer \u003ccode\u003edependencySpec\u003c/code\u003e, which appends and can be placed anywhere in the element.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch4 principle of the Quarkus Platform BOM Generator is:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003col dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eVersion constraints defined in \u003ccode\u003eio.quarkus:quarkus-bom\u003c/code\u003e will not be mutated and will be dominating during the dependency constraint alignment across all the platform members.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eEvery other platform member BOM will be processed in the following way:\n\u003col dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eif it appears to be importing any version of \u003ccode\u003eio.quarkus:quarkus-bom\u003c/code\u003e, the set of the dependency version constraints included into that version of \u003ccode\u003eio.quarkus:quarkus-bom\u003c/code\u003e\nwill be subtracted from the member BOM.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eThe remaining set of the dependency version constraints from the member BOM will be organized into groups. With each group containing artifacts coming from the same origin,\ni.e. artifacts that appear to be modules of the same multi-module project release.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eFor each group of such artifacts, the generator will check whether the \u003ccode\u003eio.quarkus:quarkus-bom\u003c/code\u003e the platform is based on includes any artifacts from the same origin.\nAnd if it does, it will try to align the versions of those artifacts with the project release version used in the \u003ccode\u003eio.quarkus:quarkus-bom\u003c/code\u003e (highlighting the differences/conflicts in the generated reports).\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eIf the \u003ccode\u003eio.quarkus:quarkus-bom\u003c/code\u003e did not appear to contain any artifacts from the same origin as the group, then every other imported member BOM is checked for including artifacts\nfrom the same origin. If such artifacts are found then the newer version of those artifacts will be preferred.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ol\u003e\n\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ol\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch3 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003eBOM generator reports\u003c/h3\u003e\u003ca id=\"user-content-bom-generator-reports\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: BOM generator reports\" href=\"#bom-generator-reports\"\u003e\u003csvg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"\u003e\u003cpath d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eBesides generating the member BOMs, the BOM generator will also generate HTML reports for highlighting how the differences between the original dependency version constraints\nand the generated ones for every member. The reports can be found under the \u003ccode\u003etarget/reports\u003c/code\u003e directory of the root platform project dir.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch2 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003eExternal Maven repositories\u003c/h2\u003e\u003ca id=\"user-content-external-maven-repositories\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: External Maven repositories\" href=\"#external-maven-repositories\"\u003e\u003csvg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"\u003e\u003cpath d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eThe Quarkus Platform build is using the \u003ccode\u003e--ignore-transitive-repositories\u003c/code\u003e option from Maven.\nIt ignores remote repositories introduced by transitive dependencies.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eThis option is used to make sure we know when one of our dependencies relies on a repository that is not Maven Central.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eWhen a dependency relies on an external repository that is not Maven Central, we have to be extra careful:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eFirst discuss it with the Quarkus Core team as it is something we want to avoid\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eIf everyone agrees it is something we should allow in this specific case:\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eAdd the repository to the Quarkus module requiring the external repository (for instance, a specific extension runtime module) - don't add it to the root \u003ccode\u003epom.xml\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eMake sure you declare Maven Central first in the added \u003ccode\u003e\u0026lt;repositories\u0026gt;\u003c/code\u003e element so that we only download dependencies that are not in Maven Central from the external repository - see existing examples in the code base\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eAdd a \u003ccode\u003e.mvn/rrf/groupId-\u0026lt;REPOSITORY-ID\u0026gt;.txt\u003c/code\u003e file containing the list of authorized \u003ccode\u003egroupIds\u003c/code\u003e for this repository (one per line) - see existing examples in the code base\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eMake sure you can fully run the build with an empty Maven local repository using \u003ccode\u003e./mvnw -Dquickly -Dmaven.repo.local=/tmp/my-temp-local-repository\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch2 Nexus:\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ol\u003e\n\u003cblockquote\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eTAG=0.0.5 \u0026amp;\u0026amp; ./check-version.sh $TAG \u0026amp;\u0026amp; ./mvnw release:prepare release:perform -DdevelopmentVersion=999-SNAPSHOT -DreleaseVersion=$TAG -Dtag=$TAG -DperformRelease -Prelease,releaseNexus -DskipTests -Darguments=-DskipTests\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003c/blockquote\u003e\n\u003col start=\"2\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eThe repository will be automatically closed and released, there's nothing else to do.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ol\u003e\n\u003chr\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eIMPORTANT\u003c/strong\u003e\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eDue to the Apache process, it is class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h1 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Quarkus Platform</h1><a id="user-content-quarkus-platform" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Quarkus Platform" href="#quarkus-platform"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 <p dir="auto"><a href="https://github.com/quarkusio/quarkus-platform/tags/latest"><img src="https://camo.githubusercontent.com/3f10d7524776b236403d711d9c4f9d74a807c07b65a69274f62b3b3bab725819/68747470733a2f2f696d672e736869656c64732e696f2f6769746875622f762f7461672f717561726b7573696f2f717561726b75732d706c6174666f726d3f7374796c653d666f722d7468652d6261646765" alt="Version" data-canonical-src="https://img.shields.io/github/v/tag/quarkusio/quarkus-platform?style=for-the-badge" style="max-width: 100%;"></a>
<a href="https://dev.azure.com/quarkus-ci/quarkus/_build/latest?definitionId=12" rel="nofollow"><img src="https://camo.githubusercontent.com/09197e61c03c296b933f6011608d22090bc7c111be7deacba92aa988bb3b8de6/68747470733a2f2f696d672e736869656c64732e696f2f617a7572652d6465766f70732f6275696c642f717561726b75732d63692f717561726b75732f31323f7374796c653d666f722d7468652d6261646765266c6f676f3d617a7572652d706970656c696e6573" alt="Build Status" data-canonical-src="https://img.shields.io/azure-devops/build/quarkus-ci/quarkus/12?style=for-the-badge&amp;logo=azure-pipelines" style="max-width: 100%;"></a>
<a href="https://www.apache.org/licenses/LICENSE-2.0" rel="nofollow"><img src="https://camo.githubusercontent.com/e83f9d6761cb5518d5180a21b9e75233c481a942f390dec8109570f7b272a876/68747470733a2f2f696d672e736869656c64732e696f2f6769746875622f6c6963656e73652f717561726b7573696f2f717561726b75732d706c6174666f726d3f7374796c653d666f722d7468652d6261646765266c6f676f3d617061636865" alt="License" data-canonical-src="https://img.shields.io/github/license/quarkusio/quarkus-platform?style=for-the-badge&amp;logo=apache" style="max-width: 100%;"></a>
<a href="https://quarkusio.zulipchat.com/" rel="nofollow"><img src="https://camo.githubusercontent.com/6186846b0f399117bc254f3a9069e6a131c6f47b790171b22467f6b31d10a2d9/68747470733a2f2f696d672e736869656c64732e696f2f62616467652f7a756c69702d6a6f696e5f636861742d627269676874677265656e2e7376673f7374796c653d666f722d7468652d6261646765" alt="Project Chat" data-canonical-src="https://img.shields.io/badge/zulip-join_chat-brightgreen.svg?style=for-the-badge" style="max-width: 100%;"></a></p>
<p dir="auto">Quarkus Platform aggregates extensions from the <a href="https://github.com/quarkusio/quarkus">Quarkus Core repository</a> and a set of other extensions developed by the Quarkus community
into a single development stack that targets the primary use-cases of the Quarkus platform. The Quarkus platform includes an integration testsuite to make sure the extensions included
into the platform do not create conflicts for each other and can be used in the same application in any combination.
More information about how a Quarkus platform is defined can be found in the <a href="https://quarkus.io/guides/platform" rel="nofollow">Platform Guide</a>.</p>

<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Platform coordination mailing list</h2><a id="user-content-platform-coordination-mailing-list" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Platform coordination mailing list" href="#platform-coordination-mailing-list"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">If you are a Quarkus Platform participant, it is highly recommended to subscribe to the <a href="https://groups.google.com/g/quarkus-platform-coordination" rel="nofollow">quarkus-platform-coordination mailing list</a>.</p>
<p dir="auto">It is a low traffic list which aims to facilitate the coordination of the Platform releases and to share important Platform-related changes.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Platform project</h2><a id="user-content-platform-project" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Platform project" href="#platform-project"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Quarkus platform is a project that brings various Quarkus extensions together aligning their dependency constraints to make sure they do not create conflicts for each other.
This project does not include any code at this point but it does use tools that collect the original dependency constraints of each extension as an input and generates
a <a href="https://quarkus.io/guides/platform#quarkus-platform-artifacts" rel="nofollow">set of platform artifacts</a> that represent a platform build.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Platform members</h2><a id="user-content-platform-members" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Platform members" href="#platform-members"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">A platform member represents a Quarkus extension project that includes one or more Quarkus extensions that are integrated into a platform.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Quarkus core</h3><a id="user-content-quarkus-core" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Quarkus core" href="#quarkus-core"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto"><a href="https://github.com/quarkusio/quarkus">Quarkus Core</a> is an essential member of a platform and is dominant during the dependency constraint alignment in a sense that its dependency constraints
are immutable, i.e. other platform members will be adapted to comply with the Quarkus core build and runtime requirements and not the other way around.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Platform member input</h3><a id="user-content-platform-member-input" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Platform member input" href="#platform-member-input"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Quarkus platform members are expected to provide:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>the dependency constraints their extensions require at Quarkus application build and run times (typically in the form of a Maven BOM artifact);</li>
<li>a list of Maven <code>test-jar</code> artifacts that should be added to the Quarkus platform integration testsuite (it would typically be some of the original tests from the extension project that are released
along with the extension as Maven <code>test-jar</code> artifacts).</li>
</ul>
<p dir="auto">The Quarkus platform BOM generator will perform an alignment across all the platform member dependency constraints and will generate a BOM for each platform member
that is compatible with all the other platform members.
Once all the member BOMs have been generated, the platform integration testsuite (consisting of the tests contributed by all the members) will be run against the generated platform BOMs.
As long as <em>all</em> the platform integration tests pass, the generated platform member BOMs (and a few other related artifacts), representing a single compatible development stack, can be released.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Generated platform member artifacts</h3><a id="user-content-generated-platform-member-artifacts" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Generated platform member artifacts" href="#generated-platform-member-artifacts"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">The platform project build will generate the following artifacts for each member.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h4 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">BOM</h4><a id="user-content-bom" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: BOM" href="#bom"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Member-specific dependency constraints that are aligned with other platform members. Applications developed in compliance with a Quarkus platform will be importing platform member BOMs that belong to the same platform release.
E.g. a platform may include extensions from <a href="https://github.com/apache/camel-quarkus">Camel Quarkus</a> and <a href="https://github.com/kiegroup/kogito-runtimes">Kogito Runtimes</a>, in which case a platform release will include a <code>quarkus-camel-bom</code>
and a <code>quarkus-kogito-bom</code>. Applications using Camel extensions will then be importing the <code>quarkus-camel-bom</code>, applications using Kogito extensions will be importing the <code>quarkus-kogito-bom</code> and applications using Camel and Kogito extensions
at the same time will be importing both the <code>quarkus-camel-bom</code> and the <code>quarkus-kogito-bom</code>.</p>
<p dir="auto"><strong>NOTE</strong> The ordering of member BOM imports in applications should not be significant, given that member BOMs are aligned as part of the platform build.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h4 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">JSON descriptor</h4><a id="user-content-json-descriptor" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: JSON descriptor" href="#json-descriptor"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">This artifact contains member extension metadata that is important for the Quarkus devtools used by application developers to discover extensions suitable for their projects.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h4 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Properties</h4><a id="user-content-properties" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Properties" href="#properties"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">At a minimum, this artifact includes information about the platform release. This information is used by the Quarkus application bootstrap mechanism to make sure the platform member BOMs imported by the application belong to the same
platform release and fail the build or log a warning in case that is not the case. This is done to make sure application developers do not import member BOMs that belong different platform releases by mistake.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Building and testing the platform project</h2><a id="user-content-building-and-testing-the-platform-project" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Building and testing the platform project" href="#building-and-testing-the-platform-project"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">The platform is currently configured in a single Maven POM file (the root <code>pom.xml</code>) with the exception of a few additional resource files. This POM file includes a few Maven plugin configuration generating the platform artifacts and a <code>platformConfig</code> configuration.</p>
<p dir="auto">The additional resource files are:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li><code>src/main/resources/extensions-overrides.json</code> — overrides extension metadata in the generated JSON descriptors (for example, marking an extension as unlisted). It is wired in through the <code>descriptorGenerator/overridesFile</code> element;</li>
<li><code>src/main/resources/xslt/</code> — XSL templates applied to generated test POMs, referenced from the test configuration <code>transformWith</code> element.</li>
</ul>
<p dir="auto"><strong>IMPORTANT</strong> Maven build process launched from the root project directory will generate the complete platform Maven project (during the <code>process-resources</code> phase) in the <code>generated-platform-project</code> directory. The generated platform project
should not be modified manually except for local testing purposes. The project will be re-generated on every platform build launched from the root platform project directory.</p>
<p dir="auto">The <code>generated-platform-project</code> will refer to the root platform project <code>pom.xml</code> as its parent POM and so may inherit properties and other common configuration from it.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Generating the platform project</h3><a id="user-content-generating-the-platform-project" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Generating the platform project" href="#generating-the-platform-project"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto"><code>./mvnw -Dsync</code> will generate the complete platform project.</p>
<p dir="auto">The platform project will typically be generated on every build anyway. But this command could be used in case you want to simply (re-)generate the platform project w/o running any other commands on it.</p>
<p dir="auto"><strong>NOTE</strong> the way it's currently done is any command launched from the platform project's root dir will be passed to the <code>generated-platform-project</code>, which means <code>./mvnw -Dsync</code> will not only generate the platform project but will also be executed against it.</p>
<p dir="auto"><strong>IMPORTANT</strong> the contents of <code>generated-platform-project</code> are committed to the repository. After changing the root <code>pom.xml</code>, run <code>./mvnw -Dsync</code> and commit the regenerated files along with the change. CI runs the generation separately and fails the build if the committed <code>generated-platform-project</code> differs from what the current configuration produces.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Installing the platform</h3><a id="user-content-installing-the-platform" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Installing the platform" href="#installing-the-platform"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto"><code>mvn install</code> launched from the platform project's root directory will (re-)generate the <code>generated-platform-project</code>, build, test and (assuming the tests have passed) install the generated platform artifacts into the local Maven repository.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Testing the platform</h3><a id="user-content-testing-the-platform" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Testing the platform" href="#testing-the-platform"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto"><code>mvn test</code> or <code>mvn verify</code> launched from the root platform project directory will (re-)generate the <code>generated-platform-project</code> and execute all the JVM tests of all the members.</p>
<p dir="auto"><code>mvn verify -Dnative</code> launched from the root platform project directory will (re-)generate the <code>generated-platform-project</code> and execute all the JVM and native tests of all the members.</p>
<p dir="auto">Once the platform project has been generated and installed in the local Maven repository, platform contributors can analyze the artifacts generated for their extensions and run their testsuite in isolation from the rest of the platform testsuite
by navigating to the desired test module and using <code>mvn</code> commands they typically would use to run their extension tests.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Platform configuration</h2><a id="user-content-platform-configuration" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Platform configuration" href="#platform-configuration"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">The platform is currently configured in the root POM file under the <code>platformConfig</code> element of the <code>io.quarkus:quarkus-platform-bom-maven-plugin</code> configuration.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Release configuration</h3><a id="user-content-release-configuration" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Release configuration" href="#release-configuration"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">The platform release configuration is expressed under the <code>release</code> element, e.g.</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-text-xml notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="  &lt;platformConfig&gt;
    &lt;!-- the platform release info --&gt;
    &lt;release&gt;
      &lt;!-- the platform (dev stack) key (if not specified, defaults to the root project's groupId) --&gt;
      &lt;platformKey&gt;${project.groupId}&lt;/platformKey&gt;
      &lt;!-- platform stream id, representing the flow of backward compatible releases
           (if not specified, defaults to the project's major.minor platform version) --&gt;
      &lt;stream&gt;2.0&lt;/stream&gt;
      &lt;!-- platform (comparable) version, must be unique in the scope of the stream
           (if not specified, defaults to the micro version of the complete platform version) --&gt;
      &lt;version&gt;0&lt;/version&gt;
    &lt;/release&gt;"><pre>  &lt;<span class="pl-ent">platformConfig</span>&gt;
    <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">&lt;!--</span> the platform release info <span class="pl-c">--&gt;</span></span>
    &lt;<span class="pl-ent">release</span>&gt;
      <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">&lt;!--</span> the platform (dev stack) key (if not specified, defaults to the root project's groupId) <span class="pl-c">--&gt;</span></span>
      &lt;<span class="pl-ent">platformKey</span>&gt;${project.groupId}&lt;/<span class="pl-ent">platformKey</span>&gt;
      <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">&lt;!--</span> platform stream id, representing the flow of backward compatible releases</span>
<span class="pl-c">           (if not specified, defaults to the project's major.minor platform version) <span class="pl-c">--&gt;</span></span>
      &lt;<span class="pl-ent">stream</span>&gt;2.0&lt;/<span class="pl-ent">stream</span>&gt;
      <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">&lt;!--</span> platform (comparable) version, must be unique in the scope of the stream</span>
<span class="pl-c">           (if not specified, defaults to the micro version of the complete platform version) <span class="pl-c">--&gt;</span></span>
      &lt;<span class="pl-ent">version</span>&gt;0&lt;/<span class="pl-ent">version</span>&gt;
    &lt;/<span class="pl-ent">release</span>&gt;</pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">The universal platform</h3><a id="user-content-the-universal-platform" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: The universal platform" href="#the-universal-platform"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">This is the BOM that contains the dependency version constraints of all the platform members. In Quarkus 1.x it is known as the <code>quarkus-universe-bom</code>.
Its coordinates can be configured using the <code>bom</code> element, e.g.</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-text-xml notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="  &lt;platformConfig&gt;
    &lt;universal&gt;
      &lt;!-- coordinates of the generated all-inclusive platform BOM --&gt;
      &lt;bom&gt;io.quarkus:quarkus-universe-bom:${project.version}&lt;/bom&gt;
      &lt;!-- whether to skip installation of the all-inclusive platform BOM --&gt;
      &lt;skipInstall&gt;false&lt;/skipInstall&gt;
    &lt;/universal&gt;"><pre>  &lt;<span class="pl-ent">platformConfig</span>&gt;
    &lt;<span class="pl-ent">universal</span>&gt;
      <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">&lt;!--</span> coordinates of the generated all-inclusive platform BOM <span class="pl-c">--&gt;</span></span>
      &lt;<span class="pl-ent">bom</span>&gt;io.quarkus:quarkus-universe-bom:${project.version}&lt;/<span class="pl-ent">bom</span>&gt;
      <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">&lt;!--</span> whether to skip installation of the all-inclusive platform BOM <span class="pl-c">--&gt;</span></span>
      &lt;<span class="pl-ent">skipInstall</span>&gt;false&lt;/<span class="pl-ent">skipInstall</span>&gt;
    &lt;/<span class="pl-ent">universal</span>&gt;</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">The <code>skipInstall</code> element can be used to exclude the all-inclusive BOM from the artifacts to be installed and deployed.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Quarkus core configuration</h3><a id="user-content-quarkus-core-configuration" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Quarkus core configuration" href="#quarkus-core-configuration"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Quarkus core is an essential member of a platform and is configured in the <code>core</code> element, e.g.</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-text-xml notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="  &lt;core&gt;
    &lt;name&gt;Core&lt;/name&gt; &lt;!-- 1 --&gt;
    &lt;bom&gt;io.quarkus:quarkus-bom:${quarkus.version}&lt;/bom&gt; &lt;!-- 2 --&gt;
    &lt;release&gt;
      &lt;next&gt;${project.groupId}:quarkus-bom:${project.version}&lt;/next&gt; &lt;!-- 3 --&gt;
    &lt;/release&gt;
  &lt;/core&gt;"><pre>  &lt;<span class="pl-ent">core</span>&gt;
    &lt;<span class="pl-ent">name</span>&gt;Core&lt;/<span class="pl-ent">name</span>&gt; <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">&lt;!--</span> 1 <span class="pl-c">--&gt;</span></span>
    &lt;<span class="pl-ent">bom</span>&gt;io.quarkus:quarkus-bom:${quarkus.version}&lt;/<span class="pl-ent">bom</span>&gt; <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">&lt;!--</span> 2 <span class="pl-c">--&gt;</span></span>
    &lt;<span class="pl-ent">release</span>&gt;
      &lt;<span class="pl-ent">next</span>&gt;${project.groupId}:quarkus-bom:${project.version}&lt;/<span class="pl-ent">next</span>&gt; <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">&lt;!--</span> 3 <span class="pl-c">--&gt;</span></span>
    &lt;/<span class="pl-ent">release</span>&gt;
  &lt;/<span class="pl-ent">core</span>&gt;</pre></div>
<ol dir="auto">
<li>Name that is used to refer to the core member in the POMs and other generated resources</li>
<li>The original Quarkus core BOM coordinates the platform is based on</li>
<li>The coordinates under which the generated Quarkus core platform BOM should be installed and deployed</li>
</ol>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Platform member configuration</h3><a id="user-content-platform-member-configuration" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Platform member configuration" href="#platform-member-configuration"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Platform members are configured under the <code>member</code> element, e.g.</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-text-xml notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="  &lt;members&gt;
