      &lt;/<span class="pl-ent">defaultTestConfig</span>&gt;
      &lt;<span class="pl-ent">tests</span>&gt;
        &lt;<span class="pl-ent">test</span>&gt; <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">&lt;!--</span> 13 <span class="pl-c">--&gt;</span></span>
          &lt;<span class="pl-ent">artifact</span>&gt;org.optaplanner:optaplanner-quarkus-jackson-integration-test:${optaplanner-quarkus.version}&lt;/<span class="pl-ent">artifact</span>&gt;
          &lt;<span class="pl-ent">skip</span>&gt;true&lt;/<span class="pl-ent">skip</span>&gt;                                                        <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">&lt;!--</span> 14 <span class="pl-c">--&gt;</span></span>
        &lt;/<span class="pl-ent">test</span>&gt;
...</pre></div>
<ol dir="auto">
<li>Name that is used to refer to the member in the POMs and other generated resources</li>
<li>The original member BOM coordinates representing members build and run times classpath constraints</li>
<li>Whether the member actually participates in the platform build or not (if a member is disabled, the rest of its configuration will be ignored)</li>
<li>The coordinates under which the generated member platform BOM should be installed and deployed</li>
<li>The BOM generator detects whether the generated member BOM has changed since the previous release and records the value of the last release in which the BOM has actually changed. The value of this element is meant to be updated during the release process by the tool itself in case the BOM has actually changed.</li>
<li>Custom dependency management configuration</li>
<li>Whether to align constraints of the member BOM that appear to be originating from the same project on a common/preferred version of that project or keep the original member BOM, the default for this option is false</li>
<li>Whether to keep exclusions found in the member BOM for thirdparty dependencies shared with other members. This option allows each member to have its own exclusions in their BOMs for common dependencies. The default value is true.</li>
<li>Maven artifact groupIds the extension artifacts of this member will have. If not configured, the original member BOM groupId will be the default. This helps optimize identify extension version constraints in the member BOM.</li>
<li>Maven artifact groupIds that member has the exclusive right to manage in the platform that other members will not be allowed to override.</li>
<li>The default configuration options for all the tests of the member</li>
<li>Can be used to skip all the member tests (unless explicitly overridden in a test config)</li>
<li>Specific test configuration will reference a Maven test jar artifact containing the tests that should be run as part of the platform's IT</li>
<li>Whether the test should be skipped during the platform IT run</li>
</ol>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h4 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Platform member test configuration</h4><a id="user-content-platform-member-test-configuration" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Platform member test configuration" href="#platform-member-test-configuration"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Besides the mentioned about <code>artifact</code> and <code>skip</code> elements the following test configuration options are available:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-text-xml notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="  &lt;test&gt;
    &lt;artifact&gt;com.datastax.oss.quarkus:cassandra-quarkus-integration-tests-main:${quarkus-cassandra-client.version}&lt;/artifact&gt;

    &lt;!-- skip native test runs (by default a single test is assumed to include both the JVM and the corresponding native test) --&gt;
    &lt;skipNative&gt;true&lt;/skipNative&gt;
    &lt;!-- skip JVM test runs but do run the native test --&gt;
    &lt;skipJvm&gt;true&lt;/skipJvm&gt;

    &lt;!-- while skipping means adding `maven.test.skip` property to the config, excluding means removing the test module for the artifact --&gt;
    &lt;excluded&gt;true&lt;/excluded&gt;

    &lt;!-- exclude individual test classes from the JVM runs (a surefire/failsafe pattern) --&gt;
    &lt;jvmExcludes&gt;**/SomeTest*&lt;/jvmExcludes&gt;
    &lt;!-- exclude individual test classes from the native runs --&gt;
    &lt;nativeExcludes&gt;**/SomeTest*&lt;/nativeExcludes&gt;

    &lt;!-- use the failsafe plugin instead of the surefire one (which is the default) for the test--&gt;
    &lt;mavenFailsafePlugin&gt;true&lt;/mavenFailsafePlugin&gt;

    &lt;!-- test groups config (applied to both jvm and native tests unless the nativeGroups is present) --&gt;
    &lt;groups&gt;!native&lt;/groups&gt;

    &lt;!-- native test groups (applied only to the native groups and overrides 'groups') --&gt;
    &lt;nativeGroups&gt;native&lt;/nativeGroups&gt;

    &lt;!-- Test POM properties --&gt;
    &lt;pomProperties&gt;
      &lt;test.project.prop1&gt;value1&lt;/test.project.prop1&gt;
    &lt;/pomProperties&gt;

    &lt;!-- JVM-only system properties --&gt;
    &lt;jvmSystemProperties&gt;
      &lt;quarkus.http.test-port&gt;$${test.http.port.jvm}&lt;/quarkus.http.test-port&gt;
    &lt;/jvmSystemProperties&gt;
    &lt;!-- Native-only system properties --&gt;
    &lt;nativeSystemProperties&gt;
      &lt;quarkus.http.test-port&gt;$${test.http.port.native}&lt;/quarkus.http.test-port&gt;
    &lt;/nativeSystemProperties&gt;
    &lt;!-- JVM and native system property --&gt;
    &lt;systemProperties&gt;
      &lt;prop1&gt;value1&lt;/prop1&gt;
    &lt;/systemProperties&gt;

    &lt;!-- extra dependencies that should be added to the test application
         WHICH PRACTICALLY SHOULD BE AVOIDED --&gt;
    &lt;dependencies&gt;
      &lt;dependency&gt;io.quarkus:quarkus-jdbc-h2:${quarkus.version}&lt;/dependency&gt;
    &lt;/dependencies&gt;
    &lt;!-- extra test scope dependencies that should be added to the test application
         WHICH PRACTICALLY SHOULD BE AVOIDED --&gt;
    &lt;testDependencies&gt;
      &lt;dependency&gt;io.quarkus:quarkus-test-h2:${quarkus.version}&lt;/dependency&gt;
    &lt;/testDependencies&gt;

    &lt;!-- An XSL template that should be applied to the generated test pom.xml to customize the configuration --&gt;
    &lt;transformWith&gt;src/main/resources/xslt/camel/reserve-network-port.xsl&lt;/transformWith&gt;"><pre>  &lt;<span class="pl-ent">test</span>&gt;
    &lt;<span class="pl-ent">artifact</span>&gt;com.datastax.oss.quarkus:cassandra-quarkus-integration-tests-main:${quarkus-cassandra-client.version}&lt;/<span class="pl-ent">artifact</span>&gt;

    <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">&lt;!--</span> skip native test runs (by default a single test is assumed to include both the JVM and the corresponding native test) <span class="pl-c">--&gt;</span></span>
    &lt;<span class="pl-ent">skipNative</span>&gt;true&lt;/<span class="pl-ent">skipNative</span>&gt;
    <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">&lt;!--</span> skip JVM test runs but do run the native test <span class="pl-c">--&gt;</span></span>
    &lt;<span class="pl-ent">skipJvm</span>&gt;true&lt;/<span class="pl-ent">skipJvm</span>&gt;

    <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">&lt;!--</span> while skipping means adding `maven.test.skip` property to the config, excluding means removing the test module for the artifact <span class="pl-c">--&gt;</span></span>
    &lt;<span class="pl-ent">excluded</span>&gt;true&lt;/<span class="pl-ent">excluded</span>&gt;

    <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">&lt;!--</span> exclude individual test classes from the JVM runs (a surefire/failsafe pattern) <span class="pl-c">--&gt;</span></span>
    &lt;<span class="pl-ent">jvmExcludes</span>&gt;**/SomeTest*&lt;/<span class="pl-ent">jvmExcludes</span>&gt;
    <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">&lt;!--</span> exclude individual test classes from the native runs <span class="pl-c">--&gt;</span></span>
    &lt;<span class="pl-ent">nativeExcludes</span>&gt;**/SomeTest*&lt;/<span class="pl-ent">nativeExcludes</span>&gt;

    <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">&lt;!--</span> use the failsafe plugin instead of the surefire one (which is the default) for the test<span class="pl-c">--&gt;</span></span>
    &lt;<span class="pl-ent">mavenFailsafePlugin</span>&gt;true&lt;/<span class="pl-ent">mavenFailsafePlugin</span>&gt;

    <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">&lt;!--</span> test groups config (applied to both jvm and native tests unless the nativeGroups is present) <span class="pl-c">--&gt;</span></span>
    &lt;<span class="pl-ent">groups</span>&gt;!native&lt;/<span class="pl-ent">groups</span>&gt;

    <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">&lt;!--</span> native test groups (applied only to the native groups and overrides 'groups') <span class="pl-c">--&gt;</span></span>
    &lt;<span class="pl-ent">nativeGroups</span>&gt;native&lt;/<span class="pl-ent">nativeGroups</span>&gt;

    <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">&lt;!--</span> Test POM properties <span class="pl-c">--&gt;</span></span>
    &lt;<span class="pl-ent">pomProperties</span>&gt;
      &lt;<span class="pl-ent">test</span>.project.prop1&gt;value1&lt;/<span class="pl-ent">test</span>.project.prop1&gt;
    &lt;/<span class="pl-ent">pomProperties</span>&gt;

    <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">&lt;!--</span> JVM-only system properties <span class="pl-c">--&gt;</span></span>
    &lt;<span class="pl-ent">jvmSystemProperties</span>&gt;
      &lt;<span class="pl-ent">quarkus</span>.http.test-port&gt;$${test.http.port.jvm}&lt;/<span class="pl-ent">quarkus</span>.http.test-port&gt;
    &lt;/<span class="pl-ent">jvmSystemProperties</span>&gt;
    <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">&lt;!--</span> Native-only system properties <span class="pl-c">--&gt;</span></span>
    &lt;<span class="pl-ent">nativeSystemProperties</span>&gt;
      &lt;<span class="pl-ent">quarkus</span>.http.test-port&gt;$${test.http.port.native}&lt;/<span class="pl-ent">quarkus</span>.http.test-port&gt;
    &lt;/<span class="pl-ent">nativeSystemProperties</span>&gt;
    <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">&lt;!--</span> JVM and native system property <span class="pl-c">--&gt;</span></span>
    &lt;<span class="pl-ent">systemProperties</span>&gt;
      &lt;<span class="pl-ent">prop1</span>&gt;value1&lt;/<span class="pl-ent">prop1</span>&gt;
    &lt;/<span class="pl-ent">systemProperties</span>&gt;

    <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">&lt;!--</span> extra dependencies that should be added to the test application</span>
<span class="pl-c">         WHICH PRACTICALLY SHOULD BE AVOIDED <span class="pl-c">--&gt;</span></span>
    &lt;<span class="pl-ent">dependencies</span>&gt;
      &lt;<span class="pl-ent">dependency</span>&gt;io.quarkus:quarkus-jdbc-h2:${quarkus.version}&lt;/<span class="pl-ent">dependency</span>&gt;
    &lt;/<span class="pl-ent">dependencies</span>&gt;
    <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">&lt;!--</span> extra test scope dependencies that should be added to the test application</span>
<span class="pl-c">         WHICH PRACTICALLY SHOULD BE AVOIDED <span class="pl-c">--&gt;</span></span>
    &lt;<span class="pl-ent">testDependencies</span>&gt;
      &lt;<span class="pl-ent">dependency</span>&gt;io.quarkus:quarkus-test-h2:${quarkus.version}&lt;/<span class="pl-ent">dependency</span>&gt;
    &lt;/<span class="pl-ent">testDependencies</span>&gt;

    <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">&lt;!--</span> An XSL template that should be applied to the generated test pom.xml to customize the configuration <span class="pl-c">--&gt;</span></span>
    &lt;<span class="pl-ent">transformWith</span>&gt;src/main/resources/xslt/camel/reserve-network-port.xsl&lt;/<span class="pl-ent">transformWith</span>&gt;</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">Surefire and Failsafe are additionally configured platform-wide to exclude every test tagged with <code>@Tag("quarkus-platform-ignore")</code>, so a member can opt an individual test out of the platform IT run from its own source tree.</p>
<p dir="auto"><strong>NOTE</strong> a property reference that is meant to end up <em>unresolved</em> in the generated test POM (so that it is resolved later, in the context of the generated project) has to escape its <code>$</code>, otherwise Maven would interpolate it while reading the root <code>pom.xml</code>. The root POM defines a <code>dollarSign</code> property for that purpose, e.g. <code>${dollarSign}${dollarSign}{platform.groupId}</code> is written into the generated POM as <code>${platform.groupId}</code>.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h4 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Platform members without a BOM</h4><a id="user-content-platform-members-without-a-bom" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Platform members without a BOM" href="#platform-members-without-a-bom"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Platform members are highly encouraged to provide their dependency constraints that represent their runtime and build time
classpath requirements in the form of a Maven BOM artifact. If a BOM does not exist yet, it's possible to configure
the dependency constraints directly in the member config, e.g.</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-text-xml notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="  &lt;member&gt;
    &lt;name&gt;Debezium-Outbox&lt;/name&gt;
    &lt;dependencyManagement&gt;
      &lt;dependency&gt;io.debezium:debezium-quarkus-outbox:${debezium-quarkus-outbox.version}&lt;/dependency&gt;
      &lt;dependency&gt;io.debezium:debezium-quarkus-outbox-deployment:${debezium-quarkus-outbox.version}&lt;/dependency&gt;
    &lt;/dependencyManagement&gt;
..."><pre>  &lt;<span class="pl-ent">member</span>&gt;
    &lt;<span class="pl-ent">name</span>&gt;Debezium-Outbox&lt;/<span class="pl-ent">name</span>&gt;
    &lt;<span class="pl-ent">dependencyManagement</span>&gt;
      &lt;<span class="pl-ent">dependency</span>&gt;io.debezium:debezium-quarkus-outbox:${debezium-quarkus-outbox.version}&lt;/<span class="pl-ent">dependency</span>&gt;
      &lt;<span class="pl-ent">dependency</span>&gt;io.debezium:debezium-quarkus-outbox-deployment:${debezium-quarkus-outbox.version}&lt;/<span class="pl-ent">dependency</span>&gt;
    &lt;/<span class="pl-ent">dependencyManagement</span>&gt;
...</pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Overriding dependency versions</h3><a id="user-content-overriding-dependency-versions" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Overriding dependency versions" href="#overriding-dependency-versions"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">There are two mechanisms for influencing the versions that end up in a generated BOM.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h4 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Member <code>dependencyManagement</code></h4><a id="user-content-member-dependencymanagement" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Member dependencyManagement" href="#member-dependencymanagement"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Constraints configured under a member's (or the <code>core</code> member's) <code>dependencyManagement</code> are prepended to that member's input constraints, <em>before</em> its own <code>bom</code> is imported. They therefore both <strong>add</strong> constraints the member BOM does not have and <strong>override</strong> versions that it does have.</p>
<p dir="auto">Three syntactic forms are accepted:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-text-xml notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="  &lt;dependencyManagement&gt;
    &lt;!-- 1. plain coordinates --&gt;
    &lt;dependency&gt;org.acme:acme:${acme.version}&lt;/dependency&gt;

    &lt;!-- 2. expanded form, supports scope and exclusions --&gt;
    &lt;dependencySpec&gt;
      &lt;artifact&gt;org.acme:foo:${acme.version}&lt;/artifact&gt;
      &lt;exclusions&gt;
        &lt;exclusion&gt;org.acme:bar&lt;/exclusion&gt;
      &lt;/exclusions&gt;
    &lt;/dependencySpec&gt;

    &lt;!-- 3. importing a third-party BOM --&gt;
    &lt;dependencySpec&gt;
      &lt;artifact&gt;com.third-party:their-bom::pom:2.0&lt;/artifact&gt;
      &lt;scope&gt;import&lt;/scope&gt;
    &lt;/dependencySpec&gt;
  &lt;/dependencyManagement&gt;"><pre>  &lt;<span class="pl-ent">dependencyManagement</span>&gt;
    <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">&lt;!--</span> 1. plain coordinates <span class="pl-c">--&gt;</span></span>
    &lt;<span class="pl-ent">dependency</span>&gt;org.acme:acme:${acme.version}&lt;/<span class="pl-ent">dependency</span>&gt;

    <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">&lt;!--</span> 2. expanded form, supports scope and exclusions <span class="pl-c">--&gt;</span></span>
    &lt;<span class="pl-ent">dependencySpec</span>&gt;
      &lt;<span class="pl-ent">artifact</span>&gt;org.acme:foo:${acme.version}&lt;/<span class="pl-ent">artifact</span>&gt;
      &lt;<span class="pl-ent">exclusions</span>&gt;
        &lt;<span class="pl-ent">exclusion</span>&gt;org.acme:bar&lt;/<span class="pl-ent">exclusion</span>&gt;
      &lt;/<span class="pl-ent">exclusions</span>&gt;
    &lt;/<span class="pl-ent">dependencySpec</span>&gt;

    <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">&lt;!--</span> 3. importing a third-party BOM <span class="pl-c">--&gt;</span></span>
    &lt;<span class="pl-ent">dependencySpec</span>&gt;
      &lt;<span class="pl-ent">artifact</span>&gt;com.third-party:their-bom::pom:2.0&lt;/<span class="pl-ent">artifact</span>&gt;
      &lt;<span class="pl-ent">scope</span>&gt;import&lt;/<span class="pl-ent">scope</span>&gt;
    &lt;/<span class="pl-ent">dependencySpec</span>&gt;
  &lt;/<span class="pl-ent">dependencyManagement</span>&gt;</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">An imported BOM is expanded, so every constraint it manages is merged into the member's input constraints. This is the preferred way to move a whole third-party dependency set (e.g. Jackson) ahead of the version Quarkus core ships — configure it on the <code>core</code> member. It is equivalent to, but much less brittle than, listing every single artifact under <code>enforcedDependencies</code> (see below).</p>
<p dir="auto"><strong>IMPORTANT</strong> the <code>artifact</code> element is parsed as <code>groupId:artifactId:classifier:type:version</code>. The accepted shorter forms are <code>groupId:artifactId:version</code> and <code>groupId:artifactId:classifier:version</code> — note that a four-segment string sets the <em>classifier</em>, not the type. A BOM import therefore has to use the five-segment form with an empty classifier: <code>com.third-party:their-bom::pom:2.0</code>. Writing <code>com.third-party:their-bom:pom:2.0</code> would try to resolve <code>their-bom-2.0-pom.jar</code> and fail.</p>
<p dir="auto"><strong>NOTE</strong> constraints pulled in through a BOM import are still subject to the normal alignment rules described in <a href="#platform-bom-generation">Platform BOM generation</a> — the <code>io.quarkus:quarkus-bom</code> constraints win, and across members the newer version of a given origin is preferred. Importing an <em>older</em> third-party BOM will therefore not necessarily downgrade an artifact that another member manages at a newer version.</p>
<p dir="auto">The expanded form can also be spelled as a <code>dependencies</code>/<code>dependency</code> list:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-text-xml notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="  &lt;dependencyManagement&gt;
    &lt;dependencies&gt;
      &lt;dependency&gt;
        &lt;artifact&gt;com.third-party:their-bom::pom:2.0&lt;/artifact&gt;
        &lt;scope&gt;import&lt;/scope&gt;
      &lt;/dependency&gt;
    &lt;/dependencies&gt;
  &lt;/dependencyManagement&gt;"><pre>  &lt;<span class="pl-ent">dependencyManagement</span>&gt;
    &lt;<span class="pl-ent">dependencies</span>&gt;
      &lt;<span class="pl-ent">dependency</span>&gt;
        &lt;<span class="pl-ent">artifact</span>&gt;com.third-party:their-bom::pom:2.0&lt;/<span class="pl-ent">artifact</span>&gt;
        &lt;<span class="pl-ent">scope</span>&gt;import&lt;/<span class="pl-ent">scope</span>&gt;
      &lt;/<span class="pl-ent">dependency</span>&gt;
    &lt;/<span class="pl-ent">dependencies</span>&gt;
  &lt;/<span class="pl-ent">dependencyManagement</span>&gt;</pre></div>
<p dir="auto"><strong>IMPORTANT</strong> this spelling <em>replaces</em> the whole constraint list instead of appending to it, so any plain <code>dependency</code> elements configured before it are silently dropped — the build still succeeds, the constraints simply disappear from the generated BOM. Prefer <code>dependencySpec</code>, which appends and can be placed anywhere in the element.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h4 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto"><code>enforcedDependencies</code></h4><a id="user-content-enforceddependencies" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: enforcedDependencies" href="#enforceddependencies"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Configured under <code>bomGenerator</code>, these are platform-wide overrides that take precedence over whatever was resolved from any member BOM, including <code>io.quarkus:quarkus-bom</code>:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-text-xml notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="  &lt;bomGenerator&gt;
    &lt;enforcedDependencies&gt;
      &lt;dependency&gt;org.example:already-managed-lib:2.0.0&lt;/dependency&gt;
    &lt;/enforcedDependencies&gt;
    &lt;excludedDependencies&gt;
      &lt;dependency&gt;org.example:unwanted-lib&lt;/dependency&gt;
    &lt;/excludedDependencies&gt;
    &lt;excludedGroups&gt;
      &lt;excludedGroup&gt;org.example.internal&lt;/excludedGroup&gt;
    &lt;/excludedGroups&gt;
  &lt;/bomGenerator&gt;"><pre>  &lt;<span class="pl-ent">bomGenerator</span>&gt;
    &lt;<span class="pl-ent">enforcedDependencies</span>&gt;
      &lt;<span class="pl-ent">dependency</span>&gt;org.example:already-managed-lib:2.0.0&lt;/<span class="pl-ent">dependency</span>&gt;
    &lt;/<span class="pl-ent">enforcedDependencies</span>&gt;
    &lt;<span class="pl-ent">excludedDependencies</span>&gt;
      &lt;<span class="pl-ent">dependency</span>&gt;org.example:unwanted-lib&lt;/<span class="pl-ent">dependency</span>&gt;
    &lt;/<span class="pl-ent">excludedDependencies</span>&gt;
    &lt;<span class="pl-ent">excludedGroups</span>&gt;
      &lt;<span class="pl-ent">excludedGroup</span>&gt;org.example.internal&lt;/<span class="pl-ent">excludedGroup</span>&gt;
    &lt;/<span class="pl-ent">excludedGroups</span>&gt;
  &lt;/<span class="pl-ent">bomGenerator</span>&gt;</pre></div>
<p dir="auto"><strong>IMPORTANT</strong> only individual artifacts are supported here — a BOM artifact will not be expanded. To override a whole BOM's worth of constraints, use a <code>dependencyManagement</code> BOM import on the relevant member instead.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Generated platform project layout</h2><a id="user-content-generated-platform-project-layout" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Generated platform project layout" href="#generated-platform-project-layout"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">The <code>generated-platform-project</code> is a multimodule project. It will contain one module per platform member. E.g.</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="[aloubyansky@localhost quarkus-platform]$ ls generated-platform-project -l
total 48
-rw-rw-r-- 1 aloubyansky aloubyansky 1377 May 27 16:35 pom.xml
drwxrwxr-x 5 aloubyansky aloubyansky 4096 May 27 16:35 quarkus
drwxrwxr-x 6 aloubyansky aloubyansky 4096 May 27 16:35 quarkus-blaze-persistence
drwxrwxr-x 6 aloubyansky aloubyansky 4096 May 27 16:35 quarkus-camel

drwxrwxr-x 6 aloubyansky aloubyansky 4096 May 27 16:35 quarkus-hazelcast
drwxrwxr-x 6 aloubyansky aloubyansky 4096 May 27 16:35 quarkus-kogito

drwxrwxr-x 6 aloubyansky aloubyansky 4096 May 27 16:35 quarkus-qpid-jms
drwxrwxr-x 5 aloubyansky aloubyansky 4096 May 27 16:35 quarkus-universe"><pre>[aloubyansky@localhost quarkus-platform]$ ls generated-platform-project -l
total 48
-rw-rw-r-- 1 aloubyansky aloubyansky 1377 May 27 16:35 pom.xml
drwxrwxr-x 5 aloubyansky aloubyansky 4096 May 27 16:35 quarkus
drwxrwxr-x 6 aloubyansky aloubyansky 4096 May 27 16:35 quarkus-blaze-persistence
drwxrwxr-x 6 aloubyansky aloubyansky 4096 May 27 16:35 quarkus-camel
drwxrwxr-x 6 aloubyansky aloubyansky 4096 May 27 16:35 quarkus-cassandra
drwxrwxr-x 6 aloubyansky aloubyansky 4096 May 27 16:35 quarkus-debezium
drwxrwxr-x 6 aloubyansky aloubyansky 4096 May 27 16:35 quarkus-hazelcast
drwxrwxr-x 6 aloubyansky aloubyansky 4096 May 27 16:35 quarkus-kogito
drwxrwxr-x 3 aloubyansky aloubyansky 4096 May 28 08:32 quarkus-maven-plugin
drwxrwxr-x 6 aloubyansky aloubyansky 4096 May 27 16:35 quarkus-optaplanner
drwxrwxr-x 6 aloubyansky aloubyansky 4096 May 27 16:35 quarkus-qpid-jms
drwxrwxr-x 5 aloubyansky aloubyansky 4096 May 27 16:35 quarkus-universe</pre></div>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>The <code>quarkus</code> module represents the <a href="https://github.com/quarkusio/quarkus">Quarkus Core</a> member.</li>
<li>The <code>quarkus-maven-plugin</code> module re-publishes the <code>io.quarkus:quarkus-maven-plugin</code> from the <a href="https://github.com/quarkusio/quarkus">Quarkus Core</a> under the platform's Maven groupId and version to simplify configurations of Quarkus application using the platform.</li>
<li>The <code>quarkus-universe</code> module represents the legacy <code>io.quarkus:quarkus-universe-bom</code> platform.</li>
</ul>
<p dir="auto">Other modules above represent actual platform members. Every member module will have the same layout, e.g.</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="[aloubyansky@localhost quarkus-platform]$ ls generated-platform-project/quarkus-camel -l
total 28
drwxrwxr-x   2 aloubyansky aloubyansky  4096 May 28 08:32 bom
drwxrwxr-x   3 aloubyansky aloubyansky  4096 May 28 08:32 descriptor
drwxrwxr-x 121 aloubyansky aloubyansky 12288 May 28 08:32 integration-tests
-rw-rw-r--   1 aloubyansky aloubyansky   767 May 27 16:35 pom.xml
drwxrwxr-x   4 aloubyansky aloubyansky  4096 May 27 16:35 properties"><pre>[aloubyansky@localhost quarkus-platform]$ ls generated-platform-project/quarkus-camel -l
total 28
drwxrwxr-x   2 aloubyansky aloubyansky  4096 May 28 08:32 bom
drwxrwxr-x   3 aloubyansky aloubyansky  4096 May 28 08:32 descriptor
drwxrwxr-x 121 aloubyansky aloubyansky 12288 May 28 08:32 integration-tests
-rw-rw-r--   1 aloubyansky aloubyansky   767 May 27 16:35 pom.xml
drwxrwxr-x   4 aloubyansky aloubyansky  4096 May 27 16:35 properties</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">The <code>bom</code> module will contain a member-specific generate Maven BOM that is aligned with all the other generated member BOMs. This BOM will be a part of the platform release.</p>
<p dir="auto">The <code>descriptor</code> module will contain a JSON artifact in its <code>target</code> directory which is generated from the member BOM artifact and will be a part of the platform release.</p>
<p dir="auto">The <code>properties</code> module will contain a <code>properties</code> artifact in its <code>target</code> directory and will be a part of the platform release.</p>
<p dir="auto">The <code>integration-tests</code> module will contain test modules generated for the tests configured in the root platform <code>pom.xml</code> for the member. There will be one module per each configured test. The tests are excluded from the platform release.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Platform BOM generation</h2><a id="user-content-platform-bom-generation" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Platform BOM generation" href="#platform-bom-generation"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">The basic principle of the Quarkus Platform BOM Generator is:</p>
<ol dir="auto">
<li>Version constraints defined in <code>io.quarkus:quarkus-bom</code> will not be mutated and will be dominating during the dependency constraint alignment across all the platform members.</li>
<li>Every other platform member BOM will be processed in the following way:
<ol dir="auto">
<li>if it appears to be importing any version of <code>io.quarkus:quarkus-bom</code>, the set of the dependency version constraints included into that version of <code>io.quarkus:quarkus-bom</code>
will be subtracted from the member BOM.</li>
<li>The remaining set of the dependency version constraints from the member BOM will be organized into groups. With each group containing artifacts coming from the same origin,
i.e. artifacts that appear to be modules of the same multi-module project release.</li>
<li>For each group of such artifacts, the generator will check whether the <code>io.quarkus:quarkus-bom</code> the platform is based on includes any artifacts from the same origin.
And if it does, it will try to align the versions of those artifacts with the project release version used in the <code>io.quarkus:quarkus-bom</code> (highlighting the differences/conflicts in the generated reports).</li>
<li>If the <code>io.quarkus:quarkus-bom</code> did not appear to contain any artifacts from the same origin as the group, then every other imported member BOM is checked for including artifacts
from the same origin. If such artifacts are found then the newer version of those artifacts will be preferred.</li>
</ol>
</li>
</ol>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">BOM generator reports</h3><a id="user-content-bom-generator-reports" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: BOM generator reports" href="#bom-generator-reports"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Besides generating the member BOMs, the BOM generator will also generate HTML reports for highlighting how the differences between the original dependency version constraints
and the generated ones for every member. The reports can be found under the <code>target/reports</code> directory of the root platform project dir.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">External Maven repositories</h2><a id="user-content-external-maven-repositories" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: External Maven repositories" href="#external-maven-repositories"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">The Quarkus Platform build is using the <code>--ignore-transitive-repositories</code> option from Maven.
It ignores remote repositories introduced by transitive dependencies.</p>
<p dir="auto">This option is used to make sure we know when one of our dependencies relies on a repository that is not Maven Central.</p>
<p dir="auto">When a dependency relies on an external repository that is not Maven Central, we have to be extra careful:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>First discuss it with the Quarkus Core team as it is something we want to avoid</li>
<li>If everyone agrees it is something we should allow in this specific case:
<ul dir="auto">
<li>Add the repository to the Quarkus module requiring the external repository (for instance, a specific extension runtime module) - don't add it to the root <code>pom.xml</code></li>
<li>Make sure you declare Maven Central first in the added <code>&lt;repositories&gt;</code> element so that we only download dependencies that are not in Maven Central from the external repository - see existing examples in the code base</li>
<li>Add a <code>.mvn/rrf/groupId-&lt;REPOSITORY-ID&gt;.txt</code> file containing the list of authorized <code>groupIds</code> for this repository (one per line) - see existing examples in the code base</li>
<li>Make sure you can fully run the build with an empty Maven local repository using <code>./mvnw -Dquickly -Dmaven.repo.local=/tmp/my-temp-local-repository</code></li>
</ul>
</li>
</ul>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Release steps</h2><a id="user-content-release-steps" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Release steps" href="#release-steps"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<ol dir="auto">
<li>Use the Maven Release Plugin to tag and deploy to the Sonatype OSS Nexus:</li>
</ol>
<blockquote>
<p dir="auto">TAG=0.0.5 &amp;&amp; ./check-version.sh $TAG &amp;&amp; ./mvnw release:prepare release:perform -DdevelopmentVersion=999-SNAPSHOT -DreleaseVersion=$TAG -Dtag=$TAG -DperformRelease -Prelease,releaseNexus -DskipTests -Darguments=-DskipTests</p>
</blockquote>
<ol start="2" dir="auto">
<li>The repository will be automatically closed and released, there's nothing else to do.</li>
</ol>
<hr>
<p dir="auto"><strong>IMPORTANT</strong></p>
<p dir="auto">Due to the Apache process, it is possible that the Apache Camel artifacts may not be directly available in Maven central, therefore you need to add the following profile to your ~/.m2/settings.xml:</p>
